		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 1:2} % *GENESIS-1-2
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-1-2}

			And the earth was
				\footnote{%
					``Without form, and void'' translates \he{tohU wAbohU}. On 5 January 1841, in \hyperlink{EMS-1841-JS-5-JAN-1841-WC-e}{notes taken by William Clayton}, JS is recorded as saying that these Hebrew words should be translated as ``empty and desolate.'' PPP also gives this translation in his 1840 ``Regeneration,'' where he renders the verse as, ``and the earth she was empty and desolate; and darkness upon the faces of the abyss; and the wind of God was brooding over the faces of the waters'' (109). Note that the version of the verse in Moses has the original KJV language, while the version in Abraham has ``empty and desolate.'' The Moses version is from around December 1830, while the Abraham dates to March 1842. It seems likely that JS adopted the ``empty and desolate'' language after he started learning Hebrew. In Seixas's 1834 \textit{Hebrew Grammar}, the nouns \he{t*ohU} and \he{b*ohU} are glossed as ``empty'' and ``desolate,'' respectively (78).
					}%
			without form, and void;%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For a good extended (and very technical) discussion of the meaning of \he{tohU wAbohU}, see EWING 17--43. Tsumura's conclusion involves saying that the phrase has nothing to do with chaos or disorganization. Quoting from page 43: ``In conclusion, both the biblical context and extra-biblical parallels suggest that the phrase \textit{tōhû wābōhû} in Gen 1:2 has nothing to do with `chaos' and simply means `emptiness' and refers to the earth which is an empty place, i.e. `an unproductive and uninhabited place.' Thus, the main reason for the author's mentioning the earth as \textit{tōhû wābōhû} in this setting is to inform the audience that the earth is `not yet' the earth as it was known to them. As Westermann notes, `creation and the world are to be understood always from the viewpoint of or in the context of human existence.' In other words, to communicate the subject of creation to human beings it is impossible to avoid using the language and literary forms known to them. In order to give the background information, the author uses experiential language in this verse, to explain the initial situation of the earth as `not yet.''' This conclusion goes well with JS's take, though he got that from Seixas.
					}
			and darkness was upon the face of the deep. And the Spirit of God moved upon the face of the waters.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[BHS] \he{w:hA'ArE.s hAy:tAh tohU wAbohU w:.ho+sEK `al--p*:ney t:hOM w:rU/a.h 'E:lohiyM m:ra.hEpEt `al--p*:ney ham*AyiM}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἡ δὲ γῆ ἦν ἀόρατος καὶ ἀκατασκεύαστος, καὶ σκότος ἐπάνω τῆς ἀβύσσου, καὶ πνεῦµα θεοῦ ἐπεφέρετο ἐπάνω τοῦ ὕδατος.}
					\item[BSV] terra autem erat inanis et vacua et tenebrae super faciem abyssi et spiritus Dei ferebatur super aquas
					% \item[AB ]
					\item[NETS] Yet the earth was invisible and unformed, and darkness was over the abyss, and a divine wind was being carried along over the water.
					\item[WGC] The earth was still a desert waste, and darkness lay upon the primeval deep and God's wind was moving to and fro over the surface of the waters.
					% \item[ASV]

		\paragraph{Genesis 1:27} % *GENESIS-1-27
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-1-27}

			So God created man in his own image,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Augustine, DDC I:39: ``A human being is an important kind of thing, being made `in the image and likeness of God' not by virtue of having a mortal body but by virtue of having a rational soul and thus a higher status than animals.''
					}
			in the image of God created he him; male and female created he them.%

					For similar statements, see \hyperref[DC20-18]{DC 20:18},

		\paragraph{Genesis 2:8} % *GENESIS-2-8
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-2-8}

			And the LORD God planted a garden eastward in Eden;%
				\footnote{%
					The Syriac Peshitta gives this passage as, ``Then the Lord God planted Paradise in Eden from the east.'' But the editors include this note: ``See the discussion on this verse in Piet Dirksen, `The Peshitta and Textual Criticism of the Old Testament,' \textit{VT} 42 (1992), 378. He translates the Peshi\d{t}ta `from of old' and notes that this reading, because it also appears in the Targum, reflects `a Jewish tradition that Paradise was created before the world'. Sokoloff-Brockelmann gloss this phrase with `in the east' (p. 1315). Both interpretations are possible.'' So if you read this as, ``God planted a garden from of old,'' as in, God had planted a garden a long time before and then put Adam in it, the garden would have to have already been there; the world he put him on would have had to exist already.
					}
			and there he put the man whom he had formed.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-3-1}

			Now the serpent was more subtil than any beast of the field which the LORD God had made. And he said unto the woman, Yea, hath God said, Ye shall not eat of every tree of the garden?

			\label{GENESIS-3-7}

			And the eyes of them both were opened, and they knew that they were naked; and they sewed fig leaves together, and made themselves aprons.%

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-3-10}

			And he said, I heard thy voice in the garden, and I was afraid, because I was naked; and I hid myself.

		% % ---

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 3:17} % *GENESIS-3-17
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-3-17}

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 11:5} % *GENESIS-11-5
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-11-5}

			And the LORD came down to see the city and the tower, which the children of men%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					AB 1A 396 on ``children of men'': ``The term for humans is \he{b*:ney hA'AdAM} (`sons/children of humans/Adam'), which subtly evokes their first ancestor. The transgression of the human/divine boundary by the first humans is now, in a sense, recapitulated by their descendants. What once was an individual breach of boundaries is now a collective breach, as humans attempt to ascend to heaven.'' NICOT 01a PDF 390: ``In this verse the builders are styled as \he{b*:ney hA'AdAM} (lit., `sons of Adam,' or `sons of the man'), which we have translated simply \textit{the men}. Earlier they had been referred to individually as \he{'iy+s} (lit., `man,' v. 3). The shift here to a term beginning with \he{b}- allows for alliteration with the verb \he{b*AnU}. Also, the shift reduces these pretentious human beings to their real size. They are but mere earthlings.''
					}
			builded.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[BHS] \he{way*erEd y:hwAh lir:'ot 'Et--hA`iyr w:'Et--ham*ig:d*Al 'a:+sEr b*AnU b*:ney hA'AdAM}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ κατέβη κύριος ἰδεῖν τὴν πόλιν καὶ τὸν πύργον, ὃν ᾠκοδόµησαν οἱ υἱοὶ τῶν ἀνθρώπων}
					\item[BSV] descendit autem Dominus ut videret civitatem et turrem quam aedificabant filii Adam
					% \item[ASV]
					\item[AB 1A] Yawheh came down to see the city and the tower that the children of humans had built.
					\item[AHB] And the LORD came down to see the city and the tower that the human creatures had built.
					% \item[NETS]
					% \item[OTR1]
					% \item[OTR2]
					% \item[OTX*]
					% \item[OTXX*]
					% \item[OTXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	\subsubsection{Genesis 12} % *GENESIS-12
	% -----
		\label{GENESIS-12}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 12:1} % *GENESIS-12-1
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-12-1}

			Now the LORD had said unto Abram, Get thee out of thy country, and from thy kindred, and from thy father's house, unto a land that I will shew thee:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-12-4}

			So Abram departed, as the LORD had spoken unto him; and Lot went with him: and Abram was seventy and five years old when he departed out of Haran.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-18-31}

			And he said, Behold now, I have taken upon me to speak unto the LORD: Peradventure there shall be twenty found there. And he said, I will not destroy it for twenty's sake.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-18-32}

			And he said, Oh let not the LORD be angry, and I will speak yet but this once: Peradventure ten shall be found there. And he said, I will not destroy it for ten's sake.

	\subsubsection{Genesis 19} % *GENESIS-19

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-1}

			And there came two angels to Sodom at even; and Lot sat in the gate of Sodom: and Lot seeing them rose up to meet them; and he bowed himself with his face toward the ground;

				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-2}

			And he said, Behold now, my lords, turn in, I pray you, into your servant's house, and tarry all night, and wash your feet, and ye shall rise up early, and go on your ways. And they said, Nay; but we will abide in the street all night.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-3}

			And he pressed upon them greatly; and they turned in unto him, and entered into his house; and he made them a feast, and did bake unleavened bread, and they did eat.

		% % ---

			But before they lay down, the men of the city, even the men of Sodom, compassed the house round, both old and young, all the people from every quarter:

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-5}

			And they called unto Lot, and said unto him, Where are the men which came in to thee this night? bring them out unto us, that we may know them.

			And said, I pray you, brethren, do not so wickedly.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-8}

			Behold now, I have two daughters which have not known man; let me, I pray you, bring them out unto you, and do ye to them as is good in your eyes: only unto these men do nothing; for therefore came they under the shadow of my roof.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-9}

			And they said, Stand back. And they said again, This one fellow came in to sojourn, and he will needs be a judge: now will we deal worse with thee, than with them. And they pressed sore upon the man, even Lot, and came near to break the door.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-10}

			But the men put forth their hand, and pulled Lot into the house to them, and shut to the door.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-11}

			And they smote the men that were at the door of the house with blindness, both small and great: so that they wearied themselves to find the door.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-12}

			And the men said unto Lot, Hast thou here any besides? son in law, and thy sons, and thy daughters, and whatsoever thou hast in the city, bring them out of this place:

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-13}

			For we will destroy this place, because the cry of them is waxen great before the face of the LORD; and the LORD hath sent us to destroy it.

			% \scriptsize

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 19:14} % *GENESIS-19-14
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-14}

			And Lot went out, and spake unto his sons in law, which married his daughters, and said, Up, get you out of this place; for the LORD will destroy this city. But he seemed as one that mocked unto his sons in law.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 19:15} % *GENESIS-19-15
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-15}

			And when the morning arose, then the angels hastened Lot, saying, Arise, take thy wife, and thy two daughters, which are here; lest thou be consumed in the iniquity of the city.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 19:16} % *GENESIS-19-16
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-16}

			And while he lingered, the men laid hold upon his hand, and upon the hand of his wife, and upon the hand of his two daughters; the LORD being merciful unto him: and they brought him forth, and set him without the city.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

					% \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 19:17} % *GENESIS-19-17
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-17}

			And it came to pass, when they had brought them forth abroad, that he said, Escape for thy life; look not behind thee, neither stay thou in all the plain; escape to the mountain, lest thou be consumed.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 19:18} % *GENESIS-19-18
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-18}

			And Lot said unto them, Oh, not so, my LORD:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 19:19} % *GENESIS-19-19
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-19}

			Behold now, thy servant hath found grace in thy sight, and thou hast magnified thy mercy, which thou hast shewed unto me in saving my life; and I cannot escape to the mountain, lest some evil take me, and I die:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-20}

			Behold now, this city is near to flee unto, and it is a little one: Oh, let me escape thither, (is it not a little one?) and my soul shall live.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-21}

			And he said unto him, See, I have accepted thee concerning this thing also, that I will not overthrow this city, for the which thou hast spoken.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-19-22}

			Haste thee, escape thither; for I cannot do anything till thou be come thither. Therefore the name of the city was called Zoar.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]

		% % ---

			And Abraham journeyed from thence toward the south country, and dwelled between Kadesh and Shur, and sojourned in Gerar.

		% % ---

			And Abraham said of Sarah his wife, She is my sister: and Abimelech king of Gerar sent, and took Sarah.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-3}

			But God came to Abimelech in a dream by night, and said to him, Behold, thou art but a dead man, for the woman which thou hast taken; for she is a man's wife.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-4}

			But Abimelech had not come near her: and he said, LORD, wilt thou slay also a righteous nation?

			% \scriptsize

					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-5}

			Said he not unto me, She is my sister? and she, even she herself said, He is my brother: in the integrity of my heart and innocency of my hands have I done this.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-6}

			And God said unto him in a dream, Yea, I know that thou didst this in the integrity of thy heart; for I also withheld thee from sinning against me: therefore suffered I thee not to touch her.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-7}

			Now therefore restore the man his wife; for he is a prophet, and he shall pray for thee, and thou shalt live: and if thou restore her not, know thou that thou shalt surely die, thou, and all that are thine.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-8}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-20-9}

			Then Abimelech called Abraham, and said unto him, What hast thou done unto us? and what have I offended thee, that thou hast brought on me and on my kingdom a great sin? thou hast done deeds unto me that ought not to be done.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 20:10} % *GENESIS-20-10
		% % ---

	\subsubsection{Genesis 22} % *GENESIS-22
	% -----
		\label{GENESIS-22}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 22:1} % *GENESIS-22-1
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-22-1}

			And it came to pass after these things, that God did tempt%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					\he{nis*Ah}, from \hyperref[NASAH]{\hel{nAsAh}}, HALOT 702a, in the piel, meaning \#1, ``to \textbf{put someone to the test},'' meaning \#2, ``to give experience, \textbf{train},'' meaning \#3, ``to \textbf{conduct a test, make an attempt}.'' HALOT lists this use in Genesis 22:1 under meaning \#1. Compare \hyperref[DEUTERONOMY-33-8]{Deuteronomy 33:8}, about Levi, and \hyperref[DANIEL-1-12]{Daniel 1:12, 14}.
					}
			Abraham, and said unto him, Abraham: and he said, Behold, here I am.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐγένετο µετὰ τὰ ῥήµατα ταῦτα ὁ θεὸς ἐπείραζεν τὸν Αβρααµ καὶ εἶπεν πρὸς αὐτόν Αβρααµ, Αβρααµ· ὁ δὲ εἶπεν Ἰδοὺ ἐγώ.}
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[NETS]
					% \item[OTR1]

					% \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXXXX*]

			And he said, Take now thy son, thine only son Isaac, whom thou lovest, and get thee into the land of Moriah; and offer him there for a burnt offering upon one of the mountains which I will tell thee of.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

			And Abraham was old, and well stricken in age: and the LORD had blessed Abraham in all things.

		% % ---

			And Abraham said unto his eldest servant of his house, that ruled over all that he had, Put, I pray thee, thy hand under my thigh:

		% % ---

			And I will make thee swear by the LORD, the God of heaven, and the God of the earth, that thou shalt not take a wife unto my son of the daughters of the Canaanites, among whom I dwell:

		% % ---

			But thou shalt go unto my country, and to my kindred, and take a wife unto my son Isaac.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-5}

			And the servant said unto him, Peradventure the woman will not be willing to follow me unto this land: must I needs bring thy son again unto the land from whence thou camest?

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-6}

			And Abraham said unto him, Beware thou that thou bring not my son thither again.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-7}

			The LORD God of heaven, which took me from my father's house, and from the land of my kindred, and which spake unto me, and that sware unto me, saying, Unto thy seed will I give this land; he shall send his angel before thee, and thou shalt take a wife unto my son from thence.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-8}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-10}

			And the servant took ten camels of the camels of his master, and departed; for all the goods of his master were in his hand: and he arose, and went to Mesopotamia, unto the city of Nahor.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-11}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-12}

		% % ---

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 24:14} % *GENESIS-24-14
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-24-14}

			And let it come to pass, that the damsel to whom I shall say, Let down thy pitcher, I pray thee, that I may drink; and she shall say, Drink, and I will give thy camels drink also: let the same be she that thou hast appointed for thy servant Isaac; and thereby shall I know that thou hast shewed kindness unto my master.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---

			And there was a famine in the land, beside the first famine that was in the days of Abraham. And Isaac went unto Abimelech king of the Philistines unto Gerar.

			Sojourn in this land, and I will be with thee, and will bless thee; for unto thee, and unto thy seed, I will give all these countries, and I will perform the oath which I sware unto Abraham thy father;

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-26-7}

			And the men of the place asked him of his wife; and he said, She is my sister: for he feared to say, She is my wife; lest, said he, the men of the place should kill me for Rebekah; because she was fair to look upon.

		% % ---

			And it came to pass, when he had been there a long time, that Abimelech king of the Philistines looked out at a window, and saw, and, behold, Isaac was sporting with Rebekah his wife.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-26-9}

			And Abimelech called Isaac, and said, Behold, of a surety she is thy wife: and how saidst thou, She is my sister? And Isaac said unto him, Because I said, Lest I die for her.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-26-10}

		% % ---

		% % ---

			And it came to pass, that when Isaac was old, and his eyes were dim, so that he could not see, he called Esau his eldest son, and said unto him, My son: and he said unto him, Behold, here am I.

		% % ---

			And it came to pass, when Jacob saw Rachel the daughter of Laban his mother's brother, and the sheep of Laban his mother's brother, that Jacob went near, and rolled the stone from the well's mouth, and watered the flock of Laban his mother's brother.

			\label{GENESIS-29-14}

	\subsubsection{Genesis 30} % *GENESIS-30
	% -----
		\label{GENESIS-30}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 30:1} % *GENESIS-30-1
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-30-1}

			And when Rachel saw that she bare Jacob no children, Rachel envied her sister; and said unto Jacob, Give me children, or else I die.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-30-2}

		% % ---

			And Bilhah conceived, and bare Jacob a son.

		\paragraph{Genesis 30:32} % *GENESIS-30-32
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-30-32}

			I will pass through all thy flock to day, removing from thence all the speckled and spotted cattle, and all the brown cattle among the sheep, and the spotted and speckled among the goats: and of such shall be my hire.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-30-33}

			So shall my righteousness answer for me in time to come, when it shall come for my hire before thy face: every one that is not speckled and spotted among the goats, and brown among the sheep, that shall be counted stolen with me.

		% % ---

			And he removed that day the he goats that were ringstraked and spotted, and all the she goats that were speckled and spotted, and every one that had some white in it, and all the brown among the sheep, and gave them into the hand of his sons.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-1}

			And he heard the words of Laban's sons, saying, Jacob hath taken away all that was our father's; and of that which was our father's hath he gotten all this glory.

			And Jacob sent and called Rachel and Leah to the field unto his flock,

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-5}

			And said unto them, I see your father's countenance, that it is not toward me as before; but the God of my father hath been with me.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 31:6} % *GENESIS-31-6
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-6}

			And ye know that with all my power I have served your father.

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 31:7} % *GENESIS-31-7
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-7}

			And your father hath deceived me, and changed my wages ten times; but God suffered him not to hurt me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 31:8} % *GENESIS-31-8
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-8}

			If he said thus, The speckled shall be thy wages; then all the cattle bare speckled: and if he said thus, The ringstraked shall be thy hire; then bare all the cattle ringstraked.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 31:9} % *GENESIS-31-9
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-9}

			Thus God hath taken away the cattle of your father, and given them to me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		\paragraph{Genesis 31:10} % *GENESIS-31-10
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-10}

			And it came to pass at the time that the cattle conceived, that I lifted up mine eyes, and saw in a dream, and, behold, the rams which leaped upon the cattle were ringstraked, speckled, and grisled.

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-13}

			I am the God of Bethel, where thou anointedst the pillar, and where thou vowedst a vow unto me: now arise, get thee out from this land, and return unto the land of thy kindred.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-39}

			That which was torn of beasts I brought not unto thee; I bare the loss of it; of my hand didst thou require it, whether stolen by day, or stolen by night.

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-41}

			Thus have I been twenty years in thy house; I served thee fourteen years for thy two daughters, and six years for thy cattle: and thou hast changed my wages ten times.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-31-42}

			Except the God of my father, the God of Abraham, and the fear of Isaac, had been with me, surely thou hadst sent me away now empty. God hath seen mine affliction and the labour of my hands, and rebuked thee yesternight.

			And Jacob said, O God of my father Abraham, and God of my father Isaac, the LORD which saidst unto me, Return unto thy country, and to thy kindred, and I will deal well with thee:

			% \scriptsize

		% % ---

		% % ---

		% % ---

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-34-1}

			And Dinah the daughter of Leah, which she bare unto Jacob, went out to see the daughters of the land.

		% % ---

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-34-4}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-34-7}

			And the sons of Jacob came out of the field when they heard it: and the men were grieved, and they were very wroth, because he had wrought folly in Israel in lying with Jacob's daughter; which thing ought not to be done.

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-34-8}

			And Hamor communed with them, saying, The soul of my son Shechem longeth for your daughter: I pray you give her him to wife.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]

		% % ---

	\subsubsection{Genesis 37} % *GENESIS-37
	% -----
		\label{GENESIS-37}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 37:1} % *GENESIS-37-1
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-1}

			And Jacob dwelt in the land wherein his father was a stranger, in the land of Canaan.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 37:2} % *GENESIS-37-2
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-2}

			These are the generations of Jacob. Joseph, being seventeen years old, was feeding the flock with his brethren; and the lad was with the sons of Bilhah, and with the sons of Zilpah, his father's wives: and Joseph brought unto his father their evil report.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 37:3} % *GENESIS-37-3
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-3}

			Now Israel loved Joseph more than all his children, because he was the son of his old age: and he made him a coat of many colours.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 37:4} % *GENESIS-37-4
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-4}

			And when his brethren saw that their father loved him more than all his brethren, they hated him, and could not speak peaceably unto him.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-5}

			And Joseph dreamed a dream, and he told it his brethren: and they hated him yet the more.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-6}

			And he said unto them, Hear, I pray you, this dream which I have dreamed:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-7}

			For, behold, we were binding sheaves in the field, and, lo, my sheaf arose, and also stood upright; and, behold, your sheaves stood round about, and made obeisance to my sheaf.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 37:8} % *GENESIS-37-8
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-37-8}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-38-23}

			And Judah said, Let her take it to her, lest we be shamed: behold, I sent this kid, and thou hast not found her.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-38-24}

			And it came to pass about three months after, that it was told Judah, saying, Tamar thy daughter in law hath played the harlot; and also, behold, she is with child by whoredom. And Judah said, Bring her forth, and let her be burnt.

		% % ---

			When she was brought forth, she sent to her father in law, saying, By the man, whose these are, am I with child: and she said, Discern, I pray thee, whose are these, the signet, and bracelets, and staff.

			And it came to pass from the time that he had made him overseer in his house, and over all that he had, that the LORD blessed the Egyptian's house for Joseph's sake; and the blessing of the LORD was upon all that he had in the house, and in the field.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-40-13}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-40-14}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-40-15}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-40-16}

		% % ---

			But if thou wilt not send him, we will not go down: for the man said unto us, Ye shall not see my face, except your brother be with you.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-14}

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-16}

			And Judah said, What shall we say unto my lord? what shall we speak? or how shall we clear ourselves? God hath found out the iniquity of thy servants: behold, we are my lord's servants, both we, and he also with whom the cup is found.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-17}

			And he said, God forbid that I should do so: but the man in whose hand the cup is found, he shall be my servant; and as for you, get you up in peace unto your father.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-18}

			Then Judah came near unto him, and said, Oh my lord, let thy servant, I pray thee, speak a word in my lord's ears, and let not thine anger burn against thy servant: for thou art even as Pharaoh.

		\paragraph{Genesis 44:22} % *GENESIS-44-22
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-22}

			And we said unto my lord, The lad cannot leave his father: for if he should leave his father, his father would die.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:23} % *GENESIS-44-23
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-23}

			And thou saidst unto thy servants, Except your youngest brother come down with you, ye shall see my face no more.

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-24}

			And it came to pass when we came up unto thy servant my father, we told him the words of my lord.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:25} % *GENESIS-44-25
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-25}

			And our father said, Go again, and buy us a little food.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:26} % *GENESIS-44-26
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-26}

			And we said, We cannot go down: if our youngest brother be with us, then will we go down: for we may not see the man's face, except our youngest brother be with us.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:27} % *GENESIS-44-27
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-27}

			And thy servant my father said unto us, Ye know that my wife bare me two sons:

			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:28} % *GENESIS-44-28
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-28}

			And the one went out from me, and I said, Surely he is torn in pieces; and I saw him not since:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:29} % *GENESIS-44-29
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-29}

			And if ye take this also from me, and mischief befall him, ye shall bring down my gray hairs with sorrow to the grave.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:30} % *GENESIS-44-30
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-30}

			Now therefore when I come to thy servant my father, and the lad be not with us; seeing that his life is bound up in the lad's life;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:31} % *GENESIS-44-31
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-31}

			It shall come to pass, when he seeth that the lad is not with us, that he will die: and thy servants shall bring down the gray hairs of thy servant our father with sorrow to the grave.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:32} % *GENESIS-44-32
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-32}

			For thy servant became surety for the lad unto my father, saying, If I bring him not unto thee, then I shall bear the blame to my father for ever.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:33} % *GENESIS-44-33
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-33}

			Now therefore, I pray thee, let thy servant abide instead of the lad a bondman to my lord; and let the lad go up with his brethren.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 44:34} % *GENESIS-44-34
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-44-34}

			For how shall I go up to my father, and the lad be not with me? lest peradventure I see the evil that shall come on my father.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Genesis 45} % *GENESIS-45
	% -----
		\label{GENESIS-45}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:1} % *GENESIS-45-1
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-1}

			Then Joseph could not refrain himself before all them that stood by him; and he cried, Cause every man to go out from me. And there stood no man with him, while Joseph made himself known unto his brethren.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:2} % *GENESIS-45-2
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-2}

			And he wept aloud: and the Egyptians and the house of Pharaoh heard.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:3} % *GENESIS-45-3
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-3}

			And Joseph said unto his brethren, I am Joseph; doth my father yet live? And his brethren could not answer him; for they were troubled at his presence.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:4} % *GENESIS-45-4
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-4}

			And Joseph said unto his brethren, Come near to me, I pray you. And they came near. And he said, I am Joseph your brother, whom ye sold into Egypt.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:5} % *GENESIS-45-5
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-5}

			Now therefore be not grieved, nor angry with yourselves, that ye sold me hither: for God did send me before you to preserve life.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:6} % *GENESIS-45-6
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-6}

			For these two years hath the famine been in the land: and yet there are five years, in the which there shall neither be earing nor harvest.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:7} % *GENESIS-45-7
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-7}

			And God sent me before you to preserve you a posterity in the earth, and to save your lives by a great deliverance.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:8} % *GENESIS-45-8
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-8}

			So now it was not you that sent me hither, but God: and he hath made me a father to Pharaoh, and lord of all his house, and a ruler throughout all the land of Egypt.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:9} % *GENESIS-45-9
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-9}

			Haste ye, and go up to my father, and say unto him, Thus saith thy son Joseph, God hath made me lord of all Egypt: come down unto me, tarry not:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:10} % *GENESIS-45-10
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-10}

			And thou shalt dwell in the land of Goshen, and thou shalt be near unto me, thou, and thy children, and thy children's children, and thy flocks, and thy herds, and all that thou hast:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:11} % *GENESIS-45-11
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-11}

			And there will I nourish thee; for yet there are five years of famine; lest thou, and thy household, and all that thou hast, come to poverty.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:12} % *GENESIS-45-12
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-12}

			And, behold, your eyes see, and the eyes of my brother Benjamin, that it is my mouth that speaketh unto you.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 45:13} % *GENESIS-45-13
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-45-13}

			And ye shall tell my father of all my glory in Egypt, and of all that ye have seen; and ye shall haste and bring down my father hither.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					% \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

	\subsubsection{Genesis 47} % *GENESIS-47
	% -----

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-1}

			Then Joseph came and told Pharaoh, and said, My father and my brethren, and their flocks, and their herds, and all that they have, are come out of the land of Canaan; and, behold, they are in the land of Goshen.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-18}

			When that year was ended, they came unto him the second year, and said unto him, We will not hide it from my lord, how that our money is spent; my lord also hath our herds of cattle; there is not ought left in the sight of my lord, but our bodies, and our lands:

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-19}

			Wherefore shall we die before thine eyes, both we and our land? buy us and our land for bread, and we and our land will be servants unto Pharaoh: and give us seed, that we may live, and not die, that the land be not desolate.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-20}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 47:28} % *GENESIS-47-28
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-28}

			And Jacob lived in the land of Egypt seventeen years: so the whole age of Jacob was an hundred forty and seven years.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-29}

			And the time drew nigh that Israel must die: and he called his son Joseph, and said unto him, If now I have found grace in thy sight, put, I pray thee, thy hand under my thigh, and deal kindly and truly with me; bury me not, I pray thee, in Egypt:

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 47:30} % *GENESIS-47-30
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-30}

			But I will lie with my fathers, and thou shalt carry me out of Egypt, and bury me in their buryingplace. And he said, I will do as thou hast said.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-47-31}

			And he said, Swear unto me. And he sware unto him. And Israel bowed himself upon the bed's head.

	\subsubsection{Genesis 48} % *GENESIS-48

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 48:1} % *GENESIS-48-1
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-1}

			And it came to pass after these things, that one told Joseph, Behold, thy father is sick: and he took with him his two sons, Manasseh and Ephraim.

		% % ---

			And one told Jacob, and said, Behold, thy son Joseph cometh unto thee: and Israel strengthened himself, and sat upon the bed.

		% % ---

			And Jacob said unto Joseph, God Almighty appeared unto me at Luz in the land of Canaan, and blessed me,

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-4}

			And said unto me, Behold, I will make thee fruitful, and multiply thee, and I will make of thee a multitude of people; and will give this land to thy seed after thee for an everlasting possession.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-5}

			And now thy two sons, Ephraim and Manasseh, which were born unto thee in the land of Egypt before I came unto thee into Egypt, are mine; as Reuben and Simeon, they shall be mine.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-6}

			And thy issue, which thou begettest after them, shall be thine, and shall be called after the name of their brethren in their inheritance.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 48:7} % *GENESIS-48-7
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-7}

			And as for me, when I came from Padan, Rachel died by me in the land of Canaan in the way, when yet there was but a little way to come unto Ephrath: and I buried her there in the way of Ephrath; the same is Bethlehem.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-8}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-9}

			And Joseph said unto his father, They are my sons, whom God hath given me in this place. And he said, Bring them, I pray thee, unto me, and I will bless them.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-10}

			Now the eyes of Israel were dim for age, so that he could not see. And he brought them near unto him; and he kissed them, and embraced them.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-11}

			And Israel said unto Joseph, I had not thought to see thy face: and, lo, God hath shewed me also thy seed.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-13}

			And Joseph took them both, Ephraim in his right hand toward Israel's left hand, and Manasseh in his left hand toward Israel's right hand, and brought them near unto him.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-14}

			And Israel stretched out his right hand, and laid it upon Ephraim's head, who was the younger, and his left hand upon Manasseh's head, guiding his hands wittingly; for Manasseh was the firstborn.

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-15}

			And he blessed Joseph, and said, God, before whom my fathers Abraham and Isaac did walk, the God which fed me all my life long unto this day,

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Genesis 48:16} % *GENESIS-48-16
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-16}

			The Angel which redeemed me from all evil, bless the lads; and let my name be named on them, and the name of my fathers Abraham and Isaac; and let them grow into a multitude in the midst of the earth.

			% \scriptsize

		\paragraph{Genesis 48:17} % *GENESIS-48-17
		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-17}

			And when Joseph saw that his father laid his right hand upon the head of Ephraim, it displeased him: and he held up his father's hand, to remove it from Ephraim's head unto Manasseh's head.

			% \begin{framed}

		% % ---
			\label{GENESIS-48-18}

		% % ---

		% % ---
